# Agents · F · part 3

930 entries, fe-pr-author to federation-learning-recorder. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **fe-pr-author**: Synthesizes commits, diff, and spec to write the PR body (fix = symptom/cause/fix, feat = addition/core/impact blocks), detects the remote host, and creates the PR (GitHub = `gh pr create` / CodeCommit = `aws codecommit create-pull-request` / any other host stops and reports). Delegate at the PR stage after fe-review passes — the main session only gets back the PR URL. ([sh5623/fe-rail](https://github.com/sh5623/fe-rail/blob/HEAD/agents/fe-pr-author.md) · ★1)
- **fe-production-hardening-reviewer**: Sweeps the clinozy-fe production-security surface for hardening gaps — CSP header/meta parity, token-in-memory storage discipline, the single refresh path, cross-tab logout, env allowlist (diagnostics off in production), and asset-inlining/CSP build constraints. Returns a prioritized gap list. Read-only; produces a report for the user to act on, never edits files. ([albetrios/clinozy-fe](https://github.com/albetrios/clinozy-fe/blob/HEAD/agent-os/agents/fe-production-hardening-reviewer.md))
- **fe-qa**: Frontend quality specialist. E2E, a11y, visual regression, enterprise UX evidence. Prefer tests over screenshots-only. Use after UI changes. ([swcstudiospace/aimeecodes](https://github.com/swcstudiospace/aimeecodes/blob/HEAD/crates/aimee_repo/src/agents/fe-qa.md) · ★1)
- **fe-realtime**: Realtime client specialist. Streaming, SSE, websockets, cluster-aware live UIs. Mutates client transport code. Use for live updates, not static pages. ([swcstudiospace/aimeecodes](https://github.com/swcstudiospace/aimeecodes/blob/HEAD/crates/aimee_repo/src/agents/fe-realtime.md) · ★1)
- **fe-refactor-advisor**: Refactoring analysis specialist — evaluates 6 dimensions (complexity, duplication, naming, structure, patterns, type safety) with Before/After code and an impact × difficulty matrix. READ-ONLY. ([sh5623/fe-rail](https://github.com/sh5623/fe-rail/blob/HEAD/agents/fe-refactor-advisor.md) · ★1)
- **fe-researcher**: External docs/library research specialist — official Next.js/React/TS docs, GitHub Issues, Stack Overflow. Every piece of information must cite a source URL. ([sh5623/fe-rail](https://github.com/sh5623/fe-rail/blob/HEAD/agents/fe-researcher.md) · ★1)
- **fe-rev-all**: Review and fix frontend (all domains) ([lihs-ie/alpha-mind](https://github.com/lihs-ie/alpha-mind/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fe-rev-all.md))

- **fe-reviewer**: Independent review against the approved spec, the approved architecture and the repo's standards, including TDD honesty, and classification of every spec change made on the branch — of a plan (gate 2), one stage, a stage's spec changes, or the whole branch (gate 3). - **fe-reviewer**: Use this agent when code changes have been made in the `client/` directory and need to be reviewed. This includes after ANY code edit to TypeScript or TSX files in client/, during `/review-frontend` or `/review` commands when client files changed, and as part of Feature, Bug Fix, or Refactor orchestration flows. The agent enforces the CountOnMe theme system, particle usage, hook patterns, immutability, and strict TypeScript type safety.\n\nExamples:\n\n- Example 1:\n user: "Add a new screen for editing meal details"\n assistant: *implements the MealDetailsScreen component and related hooks*\n… ([Konstantin212/countOnMe](https://github.com/Konstantin212/countOnMe/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fe-reviewer.md))
- **fe-specialist**: Use for general Next.js app-router FE work on the prosiddhi-frontend web app — building or editing pages, components, hooks, and the api.ts client. Construye una feature completa en su rama a partir del brief de .dev/features/, ejecutando las tareas en orden y verificando cada una contra sus criterios de aceptacion con los comandos del perfil de stack, y aplicando la base de seguridad del stack (OWASP) por construccion. Tiene modo plan (propone sin tocar codigo; el orquestador lo invoca con sonnet), modo ejecucion y modo correccion (aplica los hallazgos del review y del gate). La invoca la skill build-pipeline. ([Lucaspadularrosa/claude-plugins](https://github.com/Lucaspadularrosa/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/build-pipeline/agents/feature-implementer.md))
- **feature-implementer**: Implementa un plan técnico ya aprobado sobre la app Angular de target-app/, escribiendo código idiomático y respetando las convenciones del repo. - **feature-implementer**: Stage 2 of the .NET feature pipeline. Implements an approved plan file — production code plus xUnit/NSubstitute/FluentAssertions tests — in Mohamed's DDD/CQRS style. - **feature-implementer**: Implements exactly one approved task in whichever platform the command resolved, by applying the shared platform-implementation methodology against that platform's implementation lane. ([OnOAppsDev/ono-mobile-dev-plugin](https://github.com/OnOAppsDev/ono-mobile-dev-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/feature-implementer.md))

- **feature-implementer**: Systematically implements each step of an implementation plan, one step at a time.
